{"amberApp":1,"files":{"/README.md":"# Lift\n\nA strength-training logger: routines, live workouts with rest timer, exercise library, history, progress charts, records and body measurements.\n\n## Screens\n- Workout (src/screens/home.tsx): routines in folders, start empty or from a routine; routine editor (routine-editor.tsx)\n- Live workout (src/screens/workout.tsx, route /workout): sets, previous values, rest timer, supersets, finish summary\n- History (history.tsx): calendar and workout list\n- Exercises (exercises.tsx): ~100 exercises, search/filter, custom exercises\n- Progress (progress.tsx): weekly charts, muscle distribution, records, body measurements\n- Settings (settings.tsx): units, rest defaults, RPE, plate calculator, demo data\n\n## Data\nAll weights are stored in kg, lengths in cm; the unit setting only changes display. Keys in data.json \"values\":\n- prefs: { unit: \"kg\"|\"lb\", restCompound, restIsolation (seconds), autoRest, showRpe, barKg }\n- workouts: [{ id, name, start, end (ms), note, exercises: [{ id, exId, note, rest, ss (superset group or null), sets: [{ id, type: normal|warmup|drop|failure, kg, reps, rpe, done }] }] }]\n- routines: [{ id, name, folderId|null, exercises: same shape as workouts }]\n- folders: [{ id, name, open }]\n- customExercises: [{ id, name, muscle, equipment, kind, secondary[], custom: true }]\n- measurements: [{ id, date \"yyyy-MM-dd\", type: weight|bodyfat|waist|chest|hips|neck|bicep|thigh, v }]\n- activeWorkout: the workout in progress (same as a workout plus routineId and rest timer), or null\n- seeded: demo data (9 weeks of push/pull/legs) was loaded\n\nBuilt-in exercises live in src/lib/exercises.ts; exId is their slug.\n","/index.html":"\n\n \n \n Lift\n \n \n
